class Solution:
    def findMiddleIndex(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        def dnc(l, r, lo, hi):
            if l == r - 1:
                return l if lo == hi else -1
            
            mid = (l + r) // 2
            right = sum(nums[mid:r])
            left = sum(nums[l:mid])
            
            var left_ind; // Undefined keyword from JavaScript
            left_ind = dnc(l, mid, lo, hi + left)
            return left_ind if left_ind != -1 else dnc(mid, r, lo + left, hi)
        return dnc(0, len(nums), 0, 0)

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def findMiddleIndex(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        def dnc(l: int, r: int, lo: int, hi: int) -> int:
            if l == r - 1:
                return l if lo == hi else -1

            mid = (l + r) // 2
            left = sum(nums[l:mid])
            right = sum(nums[mid:r])

            left_ind = dnc(l, mid, lo, hi + right)
            if left_ind != -1:
                return left_ind
            return dnc(mid, r, lo + left, hi)

        return dnc(0, len(nums), 0, 0)

Let's produce final code:

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def findMiddleIndex(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        if not nums:
            return -1

        def dnc(l: int, r: int, lo: int, hi: int) -> int:
            if l == r - 1:
                return l if lo == hi else -1

            mid = (l + r) // 2
            left = sum(nums[l:mid])
            right = sum(nums[mid:r])

            left_ind = dnc(l, mid, lo, hi + right)
            if left_ind != -1:
                return left_ind
            return dnc(mid, r, lo + left, hi)

        return dnc(0, len(nums), 0, 0)

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def findMiddleIndex(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        if not nums:
            return -1

        def dnc(l: int, r: int, lo: int, hi: int) -> int:
            if l == r - 1:
                return l if lo == hi else -1

            mid = (l + r) // 2
            left = sum(nums[l:mid])
            right = sum(nums[mid:r])

            left_ind = dnc(l, mid, lo, hi + right)
            if left_ind != -1:
                return left_ind
            return dnc(mid, r, lo + left, hi)

        return dnc(0, len(nums), 0, 0)

Let's maybe consider if there is a way to make code more concise:

from typing import List

class Solution:
    def findMiddleIndex(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        def dnc(l, r, lo, hi):
            if l == r - 1:
                return l if lo == hi else -1
            mid = (l + r) // 2
            left = sum(nums[l:mid])
            right = sum(nums[mid:r])
            res = dnc(l, mid, lo, hi + right)
            return res if res != -1 else dnc(mid, r, lo + left, hi)
        return dnc(0, len(nums), 0, 0)

Let's maybe think if there is a bug in the original code's algorithm that cannot be fixed by just changing `hi + left` to `hi + right` because the algorithm itself is fundamentally flawed? Let's prove by induction.

Define F(l,r,lo,hi) returns smallest index i in [l,r) such that sum(nums[:i]) == sum(nums[i+1:]), given lo = sum(nums[:l]), hi = sum(nums[r:]), or -1. For any i in [l,r), left sum = lo + sum(nums[l:i]); right sum = sum(nums[i+1:r]) + hi. The algorithm:
- If length 1, i=l, left sum = lo, right sum = hi, check.
- Else split at mid. For i in [l,mid), right sum = sum(nums[i+1:mid]) + sum(nums[mid:r]) + hi = sum(nums[i+1:mid]) + (right + hi). So call with hi' = hi + right. For i in [mid,r), left sum = lo + sum(nums[l:mid]) + sum(nums[mid:i]) = (lo + left) + sum(nums[mid:i]). So call with lo' = lo + left. It searches left half first. Thus correct. Good.

=== RAW ANSWER ===
```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def findMiddleIndex(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        if not nums:
            return -1

        def dnc(l: int, r: int, lo: int, hi: int) -> int:
            if l == r - 1:
                return l if lo == hi else -1

            mid = (l + r) // 2
            left = sum(nums[l:mid])
            right = sum(nums[mid:r])

            left_ind = dnc(l, mid, lo, hi + right)
            if left_ind != -1:
                return left_ind
            return dnc(mid, r, lo + left, hi)

        return dnc(0, len(nums), 0, 0)
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def findMiddleIndex(self, nums: List[int]) -> int:
        if not nums:
            return -1

        def dnc(l: int, r: int, lo: int, hi: int) -> int:
            if l == r - 1:
                return l if lo == hi else -1

            mid = (l + r) // 2
            left = sum(nums[l:mid])
            right = sum(nums[mid:r])

            left_ind = dnc(l, mid, lo, hi + right)
            if left_ind != -1:
                return left_ind
            return dnc(mid, r, lo + left, hi)

        return dnc(0, len(nums), 0, 0)
